TL;DR: 本文研究电商平台评论照片的伪造检测问题。作者构建了一个检测器并附加归因图作为证据，在186,527张图像上通过严格控制验证其性能。研究发现压缩历史而非合成本身主导了朴素评估结果：最强模型在产品分离划分上达0.9999 PR-AUC，但重编码压缩后降至0.7254。单类对齐会移动而非消除检测线索，通过两类等价的最终编码可修复。归因图方面，作者用因果方法测试其有效性：能否击败随机基线取决于检测器是否对图像做出反应。最终，没有任何方法能通过所有控制，即通过"检测器盲控制"并不是忠实的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商平台的评论照片已成为一种关键文档，平台基于其审批退款，而生成式模型将伪造此类照片的成本降至零。因此需要可靠的伪造检测器及其可解释的证据（归因图），以确保检测决策可信且可审计。

Method: 构建伪造检测器并附加归因图作为证据；在186,527张图像上评估。设置三类控制实验：(1) 操纵压缩历史（生成类重编码为真实类格式）；(2) 单类编码对齐与双类等价的最终编码修复；(3) 因果测试归因图——使用从不咨询检测器的控制（如检测器盲控制、随机基线、平凡控制、中心先验），系统性地比较扰动、梯度-CAM等多类归因方法。

Result: 最强模型在产品分离划分上达0.9999 PR-AUC，但重编码合成图像后骤降至0.7254，公共检测器变化最多0.07。单类对齐仅迁移线索位置，修复后模型对原生文件的中位合成概率为0.0004。双类等价最终编码修复了问题，三种子析因实验确认编码变化贡献了全部增益（+0.176±0.009）。但该编码仅均衡了最后阶段：取证特征仍以0.7145对0.254基线的水平区分两类。归因图方面：在第一修复检测器（96/100编辑帧被判别为真实）上，无任何图胜过随机基线；在选定的检测器上，17种方法中12种在编辑图像上、8种在生成图像上通过检测器盲控制。扰动方法在两轴上均领先，梯度-CAM无正向优势。集成的区域图两轴均通过且顶部像素AP最高，耗时12.4秒/张vs遮挡法的44.9秒。

Conclusion: 压缩历史是主宰检测性能的首要混淆因素，而非合成本身；有效的检测必须控制编码格式。归因图仅当检测器对图像真正产生响应时才有意义——若检测器失效，任何归因图都不超过随机。即使通过了检测器盲控制，也不等同于忠实的解释，作者明确指出未能演示任何真正忠实的归因方法。

Abstract: A marketplace review photograph is a document: platforms approve refunds on it, and generative models drove the cost of forging one to zero. We study that detection problem, so we build a detector and attach an attribution map as its evidence, then measure what that pair delivers on 186,527 images under controls designed to change our conclusions when something is wrong. Compression history, not synthesis, drives naive evaluation: our strongest model reaches 0.9999 PR-AUC (area under the precision-recall curve) on a product-disjoint split, yet falls to 0.7254 once we re-encode synthetics into the real class's format, while five public detectors move by at most 0.07. Aligning one class relocates the cue rather than removing it, and the repaired model then assigns native files a median probability of synthesis of 0.0004. One identical final encode for both classes repairs that, and a three-seed factorial credits the encoding change with the whole gain (+0.176 +- 0.009 PR-AUC). That encode equalises the last stage only: forensic features alone still separate the classes at 0.7145 against a base rate of 0.254. For evidence we test maps causally, against controls that never consult the detector. Whether an attribution ranking exists at all depends on whether the detector reacts to the image. On our first-fix detector, which calls 96 of 100 edited frames real, no map beats a random one. On the detector we selected, twelve of seventeen maps clear that control on edited images and eight on generated ones; perturbation leads both axes and no gradient-CAM variant shows a positive advantage. The trivial controls never clear it, and on generated images the centre prior is worse than random. Our ensembled regional map clears both axes and takes the top pixel AP at 12.4 s per map against 44.9 for occlusion. Clearing a detector-blind control is not yet a faithful explanation, and we demonstrate none.

</details>


### [68] [TOOD: Task-Aware Out-of-Distribution Score Calibration for Continual Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29592)
*Mostafa ElAraby,Samer B. Nashed,Liam Paull*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了持续学习系统中OOD检测性能随时间退化（称为OOD遗忘，OODF）的问题，发现OODF与分类性能仅弱负相关，其潜在机制不同。论文提出两种退化机制：能量检测器的置信度缺口（Confidence Gap）和特征检测器的流形拥挤（Manifold Crowding），并据此提出无需训练的后处理方法TOOD，通过对数分解和重放缓冲区统计校准来改善OOD检测。实验表明TOOD在多数设定下优于未校准的能量检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习系统面临两个挑战：学习新任务的同时保持旧任务性能，以及检测分布外（OOD）输入。后者研究较少但同样重要，作者旨在理解CL系统中OOD检测性能随训练退化的机制并提出缓解策略。

Method: 提出TOOD方法，一种无需训练的后处理方法：将logits分解为各任务的能量分数，利用重放缓冲区统计信息对能量分数进行重新校准。同时通过实验揭示两种OOD退化机制——能量检测器的置信度缺口（logit尺度随任务增多的下降）和特征检测器的流形拥挤（互补性退化效应）。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和100任务的ImageNet-1K流上实验，TOOD在多数设定下优于未校准的能量检测器，在十个CIFAR配置中九个排名第一或第二，当置信度缺口最严重时提升最大。关键发现：OODF与旧任务分类性能仅弱负相关，表明其机制独立。

Conclusion: 持续学习中OOD检测退化的大部分原因来自分数校准偏差而非判别结构的完全丧失，这表明通过合理的后处理校准可以在不修改训练过程的情况下显著缓解OOD遗忘问题。

Abstract: The primary challenge of continual learning (CL) systems is to learn new tasks while remaining performant on previously learned tasks. A similarly important though less well-studied aspect of CL systems is their ability to distinguish inputs that are unlikely to come from within the set of tasks the system has already encountered, often called out-of-distribution (OOD) detection. This paper presents several findings related to the dynamics of OOD detection in CL systems, causes of performance degradation over time which we call OOD forgetting (OODF), and proposed mitigation strategies for this degradation. Chiefly, we find the unintuitive result that OODF is only weakly anti-correlated with classification performance on previous tasks, suggesting that the underlying mechanisms producing OODF are distinct. Moreover, this effect is observed for both energy-based and feature-based OOD detection methods. Energy-based detectors suffer a drop in logit scale as additional tasks are learned, which we term the Confidence Gap, while feature-based detectors also degrade under a complementary effect we call Manifold Crowding. Motivated by these observations, we propose TOOD, a training-free post-hoc method that decomposes logits into per-task energy scores and re-calibrates them using replay-buffer statistics. Experiments on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and a 100-task ImageNet-1K stream show that TOOD improves OOD detection performance over uncalibrated energy in most settings and ranks first or second in nine of ten CIFAR configurations, with the largest gains when the confidence gap is most severe. These results suggest that a substantial portion of OOD deterioration in continual learning arises from score miscalibration rather than from a complete loss of discriminative structure.

</details>


### [69] [CoDe-SSM: Context-Detail Decoupled State Space Model for Efficient UHD Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29595)
*Jiaxu Su,Zhijian Wu,Jun Li,Bo Zhang,Yefeng Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CoDe-SSM，一种用于超高清图像复原的上下文-细节解耦状态空间模型。通过全局聚类扫描模块（GCSM）聚合K个输入依赖的聚类中心并应用选择性SSM推理以建模跨区域上下文，通过局部高频模块（LHFM）利用输入推导的高频掩码和稀疏卷积专家混合来处理聚类残差，从而在保持效率的同时显著提升复原质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UHD图像复原需要在聚合空间重复出现的退化线索与保持局部图像结构之间取得平衡。现有方法通过下采样、窗口划分或聚类标记缩减来管理计算成本，但许多方法未能显式保留难以被共享聚合表征的信息。

Method: 提出的CoDe-SSM包含两条分离通路：（1）上下文建模通路由全局聚类扫描模块（GCSM）实现，将特征聚合成K个输入依赖的聚类中心，并在固定顺序序列上应用选择性SSM推理，实现跨区域上下文共享同时将计算成本与空间分辨率解耦；（2）细节恢复通路由局部高频模块（LHFM）实现，处理聚类残差并使用输入推导的高频掩码和稀疏卷积专家混合。

Result: 在五个UHD基准和五种退化类型上的大量实验表明，显式的上下文-细节解耦策略在保持理想效率的同时显著提升了复原质量。

Conclusion: 显式的上下文-细节解耦策略能有效兼顾UHD图像复原的质量与效率，验证了将共享聚合信息与局部细节信息分离处理的有效性。

Abstract: Ultra-high-definition (UHD) image restoration must balance the aggregation of spatially recurring degradation cues with the preservation of localized image structures. Compact aggregation can reduce redundant processing but may attenuate edges, textures, and other fine structures. Existing approaches manage UHD restoration cost through downsampling, window partitioning, or cluster-based token reduction; yet many of them do not explicitly retain information that is poorly represented by shared aggregation. In this study, we propose a Context-Detail Decoupled State Space Model (CoDe-SSM) for UHD restoration, which processes aggregated context and clustering residuals in separate pathways. The context modeling pathway, implemented by the Global Cluster Scan Module (GCSM), aggregates features into $K$ input-dependent cluster centers and applies selective SSM reasoning over the resulting fixed-order sequence, enabling cross-region context sharing while decoupling computational cost from spatial resolution. The detail recovery pathway, implemented by the Local High-Frequency Module (LHFM), processes the clustering residual with an input-derived high-frequency mask and a sparse mixture of convolutional experts. Extensive experiments on five UHD benchmarks and five degradation types demonstrate that our explicit context-detail decoupling strategy yields substantial gains in restoration quality while maintaining desirable efficiency.

</details>


### [70] [FlexComposer: Unified Video Compositing from Images to Dynamic Footage with Flexible Trajectory Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29627)
*Songchun Zhang,Sitong Guo,Xianghao Kong,Pengwei Liu,Yuwei Guo,Lvmin Zhang,Anyi Rao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlexComposer是一个统一的视频合成框架，将视频合成标准化为轨迹引导的条件生成任务，可无缝集成静态图像和动态素材。通过统一规范前景表示、空间感知潜在注入和合成到真实的课程学习三大设计，在无需3D重建或可学习适配器的情况下实现高保真运动控制和环境融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式视频合成方法面临控制保真度的权衡问题：要么从静态图像中幻觉出运动，无法保留预动画资产本身的动态信息；要么缺乏细粒度的空间控制，无法沿用户自定义轨迹精确放置资产。需要一个统一框架同时解决这两个问题。

Method: FlexComposer提出三大关键设计：(1) 统一规范前景表示——解耦物体的内在运动与全局位移，将异构输入标准化到稳定的居中潜在空间；(2) 空间感知潜在注入策略——利用VAE潜在空间的平移等变性，通过无参数机制将规范特征迁移到目标轨迹上；(3) 混合数据集与合成到真实课程——结合程序化仿真、真实电影镜头和生成数据，隐式学习物理合理的照明和阴影协调。

Result: 大量实验表明，FlexComposer在视觉质量、时间一致性和轨迹遵循度上均优于当前最先进的方法，能够处理从产品照片到动态主体等多样化输入。

Conclusion: FlexComposer通过统一的轨迹引导条件生成框架，成功解决了视频合成中运动保真与空间控制之间的权衡问题，无需显式3D重建或辅助可学习适配器即可实现高保真运动控制和环境集成。

Abstract: Generative video compositing, which involves inserting external assets seamlessly into existing video sequences, is essential for content creation and visual effects. However, existing approaches suffer from a control-fidelity trade-off: they either hallucinate motion from static images, failing to preserve the dynamics of pre-animated assets, or lack fine-grained spatial control for precise asset placement along user-defined trajectories. We propose FlexComposer, a unified framework that standardizes video compositing as a trajectory-guided conditional generation task, enabling the seamless integration of both static images and dynamic footage. Our approach introduces three key designs: (1) a Unified Canonical Foreground Representation that decouples an object's intrinsic motion from its global displacement, standardizing heterogeneous inputs into a stabilized, centered latent space; (2) a Spatial-Aware Latent Injection strategy that exploits the translation equivariance of VAE latent spaces to transport canonical features onto target trajectories via a parameter-free mechanism; and (3) a Hybrid Dataset and Synthetic-to-Real Curriculum that synergizes procedural simulation, real-world cinematic footage, and generative data to implicitly learn physically plausible illumination and shadow harmonization. This unified design handles diverse inputs from product photos to dynamic subjects achieving high-fidelity motion control and environmental integration without the need for explicit 3D reconstruction or auxiliary learnable adapters. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FlexComposer outperforms state-of-the-art methods in visual quality, temporal consistency, and trajectory adherence.

</details>


### [71] [OASIS: Occlusion-aware Single-image Hand Avatar Reconstruction via 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29633)
*Zhisheng Han,Shiyao Wu,Jiayan Qiu,Yakun Ju,Lu Liu,Le Zhang,Pengfei Feng,Huiyu Zhou,Zheheng Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OASIS是一个基于3D高斯泼溅（3D Gaussian Splatting）的单图手部3D头像重建框架，通过几何对齐的视觉证据标记、可见性条件化的点-图像注意力机制以及Feature-on-Mesh表示，在保持高效渲染的同时恢复手部的精细几何细节和姿态相关形变，在视觉保真度和效率上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图像3D手部头像重建本质上是欠定的（ill-posed），且因严重的自遮挡和手部关节的复杂姿态相关形变而极具挑战。现有方法主要依赖隐式的NeRF风格表示，其体素拟合计算开销大且难以保留手部的精细细节。

Method: 提出OASIS框架，包含三个关键组件：(1) 通过将输入图像观测与3D手部几何显式对齐并上下文自适应标记化，构建几何对齐的视觉证据标记；(2) 引入可见性条件化的点-图像注意力机制，可靠地将视觉证据传递到几何标记，生成遮挡感知的高斯特征；(3) 提出Feature-on-Mesh表示，使高斯形变受局部表面拉伸引导以捕捉手部的非刚性形变。采用一次性适配方案，先从多身份训练数据中学习共享手部先验，再适配到目标图像实现特定目标的重建。

Result: 大量实验表明OASIS在视觉保真度和效率上均优于现有基线，在挑战性姿态和真实场景中表现突出，并在下游应用（如文本到头像生成、纹理编辑）中展现出强通用性。

Conclusion: OASIS通过3D高斯泼溅范式结合遮挡感知的特征传递和基于网格的形变引导，有效解决了单图手部头像重建中的自遮挡和姿态形变难题，同时显著提升了效率。

Abstract: Single-image 3D hand avatar reconstruction is fundamentally ill-posed and particularly challenging due to limited visual evidence under severe self-occlusion and the complex pose-dependent deformation of highly articulated hands. Existing methods predominantly rely on implicit NeRF-style representations, whose volumetric fitting is computationally expensive and often struggles to preserve fine-grained hand details. In this work, we present OASIS, a tailored 3D Gaussian Splatting framework for single-image hand avatar reconstruction. To faithfully encode sparse image-specific appearance cues in single-view reconstruction, we construct geometry-aligned visual evidence tokens by explicitly aligning input image observations with 3D hand geometry and context-adaptively tokenizing the resulting visual evidence. Since severe self-occlusion makes the reliability of image evidence inherently visibility-dependent, we introduce a visibility-conditioned point-image attention to reliably transfer visual evidence to geometric tokens, yielding occlusion-aware Gaussian features for faithful and robust reconstruction. To further capture non-rigid deformation of articulated hands, we introduce a Feature-on-Mesh representation to enable Gaussian deformation to be guided by local surface stretching. Under this framework, we adopt a one-shot adaptation scheme that learns a shared hand prior from multi-identity training data and then fits it to a target image for target-specific reconstruction. Extensive experiments show that OASIS outperforms existing baselines in both visual fidelity and efficiency across challenging poses and in-the-wild scenarios, and further demonstrates strong versatility in downstream applications such as text-to-avatar generation and texture editing.

</details>


### [72] [CodeShrink: Adaptive Visual Compression for Efficient Multimodal Code Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29637)
*Wenxin Tang,Jingyu Xiao,Zhenyu Liu,Zipeng Xie,Junliang Liu,Wang Luo,Yuan Jiang,Yintong Huo,Michael Lyu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CodeShrink是一个自适应的视觉压缩框架，通过空白消除渲染、基于强化学习的自适应压缩配置和指令感知的主导令牌选择三个组件，在代码问答、克隆检测和代码补全任务中最高减少71.2%的视觉令牌使用，同时保持甚至超过未压缩文本输入的模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将源代码渲染为图像可降低多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)的输入成本，但现有分辨率缩放方法忽略了两个低效来源：由换行和缩进导致的空白区域，以及与当前指令无关的代码区域。此外，最佳压缩设置因输入、任务和模型而异，固定比例的压缩策略存在局限。

Method: CodeShrink由三个组件构成：(1) Blank-Free Rendering，用紧凑布局和显式结构标记替代依赖空白的布局，消除布局引起的视觉令牌；(2) Adaptive Compression Configuration，使用强化学习训练的轻量级代理为每个输入预测平衡令牌效率和可读性的压缩设置；(3) Dominant Token Selection，在推理时联合分析指令和代码图像以剪除与任务无关的视觉令牌。

Result: 在代码问答、克隆检测和代码补全三个任务上，CodeShrink最高减少71.2%的视觉令牌使用，同时匹配或超过未压缩文本输入的基准性能，并一致优于基于文本和视觉压缩的基线方法。

Conclusion: 将布局压缩、自适应配置和指令感知剪枝相结合，可以使多模态代码理解更加高效，为MLLM处理代码图像输入提供了一种有效的视觉压缩路径。

Abstract: Rendering source code as images offers a promising way to reduce the input costs of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). Adjusting image resolution can trade visual token cost against content fidelity. However, resolution scaling alone overlooks two sources of inefficiency: blank regions created by line breaks and indentation, and code regions irrelevant to the current instruction. Moreover, the best compression setting varies across inputs, tasks, and models, limiting fixed-ratio strategies. We propose CodeShrink, an adaptive visual compression framework with three components. Blank-Free Rendering replaces whitespace-dependent layouts with compact layouts and explicit structural markers, removing layout-induced tokens. Adaptive Compression Configuration uses a lightweight agent trained with reinforcement learning to predict a per-input setting that balances token efficiency and readability. Dominant Token Selection jointly analyzes the instruction and code image to prune task-irrelevant visual tokens during inference. We evaluate CodeShrink on code question answering, clone detection, and code completion. CodeShrink reduces visual token use by up to 71.2\% while matching or exceeding uncompressed text-only inputs, and consistently outperforms text-based and visual compression baselines across all three tasks. These results show that combining layout compaction, adaptive configuration, and instruction-aware pruning can make multimodal code understanding more efficient. Our code is available at https://github.com/vinsontang1/CodeShrink.

</details>


### [73] [HierDoc: Hierarchical Page-to-Region Evidence Routing for Long-Document Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29638)
*Rongjian Gu,Wengang Zhou,Junyu Xiong,Yonghui Wang,Bing Yin,Bei Wang,Houqiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HierDoc是一个分层证据路由框架，将长文档证据获取建模为从页面到区域的两阶段集合预测任务。通过页面策略从全文档中选择证据页面，再通过区域策略选择页面中的语义元素，两个策略均使用阶段式GRPO优化。在多个基准上达到开源系统中的最优或媲美性能，LongDocURL相对最强开源基线提升16.87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多页文档VQA方法在页面级和区域级证据定位上存在割裂：页面中心方法将区域操作仅作为导航辅助，而区域中心方法假设相关页面已预先提供。页面选择与区域选择未能形成连续的证据决策序列，导致证据获取效率低下。

Method: HierDoc采用分层证据路由框架，将长文档证据获取建模为两阶段集合预测：1) 页面策略从完整文档中选择证据页面；2) 所选页面被解析为语义元素后，区域策略从中选择关键元素。两个answer-agnostic策略均通过阶段式GRPO优化，使用粒度特定的结构化集合奖励。答案模型接收选中的完整页面、区域裁剪图像以及OCR或表格文本，在保留全局上下文的同时强调细粒度证据。

Result: 在多个基准上达到开源系统中的最优或竞争力性能，LongDocURL相对最强开源基线提升16.87%。消融实验显示，选中的区域级证据较纯页面系统在准确率和F1上分别提升5.51%和4.82%。

Conclusion: 将粗粒度页面路由与细粒度区域路由组织为统一证据获取过程中连续的、分别优化的阶段，能够显著提升长文档VQA的证据定位精度和整体性能。

Abstract: Multi-page document visual question answering requires locating sparse evidence at both the page and region levels. Existing approaches typically emphasize one level over the other: page-centric methods focus on page acquisition, with region operations serving mainly as navigation aids, whereas region-centric methods assume that the relevant pages have already been supplied. Consequently, page and region selection remain disconnected rather than forming successive evidence decisions. We propose HierDoc, a hierarchical evidence-routing framework that formulates long-document evidence acquisition as two-stage set prediction from pages to regions. A page policy selects evidence pages from the full document; these pages are then parsed for semantic elements, after which a region policy selects the elements passed to a downstream answer model. Both answer-agnostic policies are optimized with stage-wise GRPO using granularity-specific structured-set rewards. The answer model receives selected full pages together with selected region crops and OCR or table text, preserving global context while emphasizing fine-grained evidence. Across the evaluated benchmarks, HierDoc achieves state-of-the-art or competitive performance among open-weight systems, improving LongDocURL by 16.87% relative to the strongest reported open-weight baseline. Controlled ablations further show that selected regional evidence improves the page-only system in accuracy and F1 by 5.51% and 4.82%, respectively. These results demonstrate the benefit of organizing coarse page routing and fine-grained region routing as successive, separately optimized stages of a unified evidence-acquisition process.

</details>


### [74] [Scaling Properties of Text Conditioning in Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29679)
*Zilong Chen,Chaorui Deng,Kunchang Li,Hongyi Yuan,Haoqi Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉生成中文本条件作用的经验性缩放特性，发现扩散损失随提示中的结构化语言量呈线性/幂律缩放，并据此通过构建结构化提示和改进prompter训练，使系统在多项基准上超越所有开源模型并匹配或超越最强闭源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉生成中文本条件作用的缩放特性很少被量化，因为扩散损失不随自然语言提示中的token数量缩放。作者希望揭示扩散损失与提示结构化语言量之间的关系，并利用该关系改进文本条件视觉生成的性能。

Method: 1) 提出两种互补的结构化语言量化指标：白盒似然指标GPG和黑盒属性指标ED；2) 通过受控训练实验发现收敛扩散损失随GPG近似线性下降、随ED呈幂律下降；3) 利用图像语义和几何标注构建结构化提示以提升diffusability；4) 通过监督微调、冷启动和验证器门控的在线策略蒸馏训练prompter以提升promptability。

Result: 系统在几乎所有组合性、推理和世界知识基准上超越所有评估的开源权重模型，在大多数评估上匹配或超越最强闭源权重模型。

Conclusion: 文本条件下的视觉生成存在可测量的经验缩放规律：扩散损失与提示中的结构化语言量呈可预测的缩放关系，利用这一规律可以显著提升文本条件视觉生成的性能。

Abstract: We study empirical scaling properties for text conditioning in visual generation. Such properties have rarely been measured because diffusion loss does not scale with the number of tokens in natural-language prompts. Surprisingly, we find that the converged diffusion loss scales with the amount of structured language in the prompt. To quantify structured language, we adapt two complementary measures: a white-box likelihood metric (GPG) and a black-box attribute metric (ED). Across controlled training runs, the converged diffusion loss decreases approximately linearly with GPG and follows a power law with ED. Guided by these scaling properties, we improve \emph{diffusability} by constructing structured prompts with semantic and geometric annotations derived from images, and improve \emph{promptability} by training a prompter through supervised fine-tuning, cold-start, and verifier-gated on-policy distillation. The resulting system outperforms all evaluated open-weight models on nearly every compositional, reasoning, and world-knowledge benchmark, while matching or surpassing the strongest closed-weight models on most evaluations.

</details>


### [75] [Toward Robust and 3D-Aware RGB-NIR Imaging in the Dark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29684)
*Muyao Niu,Mingze Ma,Yifan Zhan,Qingtian Zhu,Zhihang Zhong,Wei Guo,Chang Wen Chen,Yinqiang Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需干净RGB监督的3D感知神经模型用于RGB-NIR低光图像融合增强，通过将极端噪声RGB与NIR线索在3D空间中隐式融合，从而恢复干净RGB图像，在不同噪声水平下表现出良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGB-NIR低光成像方法依赖精心设计的数据配对并需要干净的RGB真值监督，导致对多种场景的鲁棒性受限，且数据收集成本高。作者希望摆脱对干净RGB标签的依赖，提升模型在不同场景和噪声水平下的泛化能力。

Method: 提出将3D感知神经建模引入RGB-NIR低光成像，利用三维空间中的隐式神经表示，在无需干净RGB监督的条件下，将带噪的RGB观测与NIR结构线索在3D空间中进行隐式融合，进而恢复干净的RGB图像。模型不依赖配对训练数据，能够自适应不同噪声水平。

Result: 在合成数据与真实数据上的大量实验表明，所提方法在无需干净RGB监督的前提下，相比现有方法取得了更优的融合增强效果，并展现出对不同噪声水平的良好泛化能力。

Conclusion: 3D感知神经建模为RGB-NIR低光成像提供了一种新颖且有效的视角，消除了对干净RGB数据收集的需求，提升了模型在不同噪声条件下的鲁棒性和实用性。

Abstract: Robust low-light imaging remains challenging for the community. Recent studies have explored fusing Near-Infrared (NIR) with noisy RGB to achieve improved enhancement, yet most methods depend on carefully curated training data pairs, with limited robustness under different scenarios. This paper offers a new perspective for RGB-NIR low-light imaging by incorporating 3D-aware neural modeling. Without using clean RGB supervision, a powerful model can be optimized to implicitly fuse extremely noisy RGB observations with NIR cues in 3D space, effectively recovering clean RGB images. The proposed model obviates the requirement for clean RGB data collection, generalizes across different noise levels. Extensive evaluations on synthetic and real data demonstrate its superiority. Codes available: https://github.com/MyNiuuu/3DarkFusion

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [76] [OpenClaw and Ollama in Agentic AI: Toward Fully Autonomous and Scalable AI Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28629)
*Konstantinos I. Roumeliotis,Ranjan Sapkota*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向Agentic AI的分层架构（推理、编排、执行），并基于OpenClaw-Ollama全栈系统进行实验验证，证明持久记忆、工具使用和自适应决策等能力源于系统级集成而非单模型，且性能随着架构复杂度增加而提升。研究还探讨了可扩展性、安全性、隐私、治理和评估等挑战，并开源了所有代码、模型和数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型从响应式向持久、可执行系统的快速转型暴露出Agentic AI在架构层面的关键空白，尤其是推理层、编排层和执行层的分离尚未被清晰定义，同时缺乏统一的框架来设计和评估全栈智能体系统。

Method: 提出一个全面的分层Agentic AI架构，涵盖从响应式LLM到持久自主智能体的演化路径；将OpenClaw与Ollama组合为全栈系统进行分析，其中Ollama负责LLM推理层，OpenClaw负责代理运行时编排（集成推理、工具使用和行动执行）；并进行了原型实验验证。

Result: 实验验证表明，持久记忆、工具利用和自适应决策等能力源于系统级集成而非独立模型，性能随架构复杂度增加而持续提升；同时识别出可扩展性、安全、隐私、治理和评估等关键挑战。

Conclusion: 该工作为Agentic AI建立了统一的架构基础，验证了全栈自主AI智能体的有效性，并为构建可扩展、安全、可信的智能体系统提供了路线图。未来方向包括可扩展多智能体架构、分布式自主系统以及面向人类认知的Agentic AI框架。

Abstract: The rapid transition from reactive large language models (LLMs) to persistent, action-capable systems has exposed critical gaps in the architectural understanding of Agentic AI, particularly in separating inference, orchestration, and execution layers for autonomous AI agents. Despite recent advances, unified frameworks for designing and evaluating full-stack agentic systems remain limited. This paper presents a comprehensive, layered architecture for Agentic AI, outlining the evolution from reactive LLM interfaces to persistent, goal-driven autonomous AI agents with memory, planning, and continuous execution. We analyze OpenClaw and Ollama as a full-stack Agentic AI system, where Ollama serves as the LLM inference layer and OpenClaw enables agent runtime orchestration, integrating reasoning, tool use, and action execution. A prototype experimental validation of the OpenClaw-Ollama architecture demonstrates that capabilities such as persistent memory, tool utilization, and adaptive decision-making emerge from system-level integration rather than standalone models, with performance improving consistently as architectural complexity increases. The study further examines challenges in scalability, security, privacy, governance, and evaluation of agentic systems, highlighting the need for robust benchmarking and system-level design. Future directions include scalable multi-agent architectures, distributed autonomous systems, and human-aware Agentic AI frameworks for responsible deployment. Overall, this work establishes a unified architectural foundation for Agentic AI, validates the effectiveness of full-stack autonomous AI agents, and provides a roadmap for building scalable, secure, and trustworthy agentic systems. All models, code, and datasets are publicly released to support reproducibility and benchmarking.

</details>


### [77] [Can AI Evaluate AI Scientists? A Benchmarking Study of Autonomous Research Generation Systems Using Automated Multi-Model Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28631)
*Vaibhava Lakshmi Ravideshik,Mayank Kejriwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个用于评估AI Scientist系统生成论文质量的自动化基准测试协议，利用前沿LLM作为同行评审者，从原创性、科学严谨性、清晰度和重要性四个维度评估论文。试验覆盖Sakana AI v1/v2、CycleResearcher、Data-to-Paper及FARS共60篇论文，发现FARS显著优于其他系统，且Gemini和Claude评审一致性极高（ρ=0.907），验证了多模型LLM评审的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI Scientist系统能自动化科学发现，但评估和比较AI生成论文质量仍是一个开放挑战。现有方法缺乏统一的、可量化的评估基准，无法公平比较不同AI研究框架的输出质量，需要建立标准化的评估协议。

Method: 设计了一个自动化同行评审基准协议：使用三个独立的前沿LLM评审者（GPT-5.4、Gemini、Claude），从原创性、科学严谨性、清晰度和重要性四个维度对论文打分（1-5分制）。四个AI Scientist框架（Sakana AI v1/v2、CycleResearcher、Data-to-Paper）各自运行在一致的15个研究提案上生成60篇论文，与FARS的15篇基准论文一起评估，并通过Spearman相关系数分析评审者间一致性。

Result: FARS基准论文在四个框架中表现最佳（平均分2.14-2.47），远超其他系统（1.00-1.87），在Gemini和Claude评审上分数是次优系统的2倍以上。Gemini与Claude评审高度一致（ρ=0.907, p<0.001），两者与综合得分极强相关（ρ=0.961）。但GPT-5.4与其他评审者一致性较弱（ρ≈0.32），表明其评估标准不同。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了首个AI Scientist系统的定量基准，证明多模型LLM评估能提供可扩展、一致的自动化论文质量评估框架。但GPT-5.4的弱一致性提示不同LLM评审标准存在差异，未来需要进一步校准评审模型以构建更统一的评估体系。

Abstract: AI Scientist systems capable of autonomous research have the potential to significantly accelerate scientific discovery. However, evaluating and comparing the quality of AI-generated papers remains an open challenge. We propose and implement a rigorous benchmarking protocol using an automated peer-review system that harnesses frontier large language models to assess scientific papers across four core dimensions: originality, scientific rigor, clarity, and significance. We evaluate four leading AI Scientist frameworks: \textit{Sakana AI (v1 & v2)}, \textit{CycleResearcher}, and \textit{Data-to-Paper}. Each framework was run on a consistent set of 15 research proposals published by a commercial autonomous AI scientist company (FARS), generating 60 papers that we evaluate alongside 15 FARS benchmark papers. Using three independent LLM reviewers (GPT-5.4, Gemini, and Claude), we find that FARS benchmark papers significantly outperform all competing frameworks, achieving mean scores of 2.14--2.47 on a 1--5 scale compared to 1.00--1.87 for other systems. Notably, FARS scores are more than 2$\times$ higher than the next-best systems on Gemini and Claude evaluations. We find strong agreement among Gemini and Claude ($ρ$ = 0.907, $p < 0.001$), and both correlate extremely strongly with the synthesis score ($ρ$ = 0.961, $p < 0.001$), validating the reliability of automated evaluation. However, GPT-5.4 exhibits weaker agreement ($ρ\approx 0.32$), suggesting it evaluates papers using different criteria. These results establish the first quantitative benchmark for AI Scientist systems and demonstrate that multi-model LLM evaluation provides a scalable, consistent framework for assessing autonomous research quality.

</details>


### [78] [LLM Framework for Discovering Major Mathematical Conjectures: AI's Quest for the Next Riemann Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28632)
*Alizer Wong,Zixin Zeng,Yi Tan,Wenyuan Li,Xuhang Chen,Xingru Lai,Yang Shi,Liangsi Lu,Yanhui Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个三阶段流水线用于重大数学猜想的系统化发现与验证：区域搜索从局部证据模块生成候选猜想，反思性验证评估其基础性、新颖性和潜在意义，最终在Lean 4和Mathlib中进行形式化验证。实验显示二十个候选猜想全部通过Lean解析和类型检查，且无重复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重大数学猜想仍高度依赖专家直觉，目前缺乏一种统一的方法来系统性地生成和验证具有实质数学潜力的猜想。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过自动化手段发现具有"高问题品味"的数学问题。

Method: 提出三阶段流水线：(1) 区域搜索（region search）：从显式局部证据模块中探索候选猜想；(2) 反思性验证（reflective validation）：评估候选的基础性、新颖性和潜在重要性；(3) 形式化验证（formal validation）：在Lean 4和Mathlib中进行机器可检查的证明验证。

Result: 在二十个候选猜想上的实验表明：全部二十个候选通过Lean解析和类型检查；全部二十个候选未被exact?直接吸收；全部二十个候选未被aesop自动消解；且没有显式重复或近似重复。

Conclusion: 该流水线能够从自然语言稳定地过渡到形式化检查，系统性地生成和验证具有实质数学潜力的猜想，为减少对专家直觉的依赖、辅助人类数学研究提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Major mathematical conjectures still depend heavily on expert intuition, so a unified method for the systematic generation and validation of conjectures with substantial mathematical potential remains unavailable. We present a three stage pipeline for major conjecture discovery, with region search from explicit local evidence modules, reflective validation for foundationality, novelty, and potential significance, and formal validation in Lean 4 and Mathlib. The objective is the discovery of mathematical problems with high problem taste, namely problems whose proofs could reorganize the language of a research area and provide durable help to human mathematical research. Experiments on twenty candidates showstable passage from natural language to formal checks, with twenty out of twenty candidates passing Lean parsing and type checking, twenty out of twenty candidates not directly absorbed by exact?,twenty out of twenty candidates not automatically discharged by aesop, and no explicit duplicates or near duplicates.

</details>


### [79] [ThinkReset: Learnable Intermediate Interface Construction for Bounded-Context Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28642)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ThinkReset框架，解决长链推理在受限上下文窗口下的核心瓶颈——缺乏可复用的中间接口来替代被丢弃的历史信息，并针对结果奖励驱动的长链强化学习中"过早猜测"失败模式进行直接优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长链推理虽能提升复杂问题表现，但引入冗余积累、上下文溢出和错误锚定等新问题。现有工作关注轨迹压缩或测试时控制，但作者认为在受限上下文窗口下真正的瓶颈是可复用中间接口的缺失——该接口能替代被丢弃的历史并支持继续求解。此外，结果奖励驱动的长链RL存在关键失败模式：当模型在窗口耗尽前未解出任务时，最终答案奖励会鼓励过早猜测而非继续仔细推理。

Method: ThinkReset在文本空间中实现了上述视角：通过接口回写（interface writeback）和重置（reset）显式构建可复用的中间接口，并直接优化重置后的延续成功率。具体包括两个关键组件：(1) 将求解过程中的关键中间状态显式写入为可复用接口；(2) 在上下文窗口受限时重置并利用该接口继续求解，而非被历史冗余拖累。

Result: 在多个长地平线推理基准上，ThinkReset在固定上下文窗口下一致地提升了成功率。

Conclusion: 研究论证了在受限上下文中，可复用中间接口的缺失而非轨迹压缩或测试时控制才是核心瓶颈；ThinkReset通过文本空间中的接口写回与重置策略，并直接优化重置后延续成功率，有效应对了长链推理中的上下文溢出和过早猜测问题。

Abstract: Long chain-of-thought reasoning improves performance on complex problems, but it also introduces redundancy accumulation, context overflow, and error anchoring. We argue that under bounded context windows, the core bottleneck is not trajectory compression or test-time control, but the absence of a reusable intermediate interface that can replace discarded history and support continued solving. We further identify a key failure mode of outcome-reward-driven long-chain reinforcement learning: when the model has not solved the task before the window is nearly exhausted, the final-answer reward encourages premature guessing rather than continued careful reasoning. We propose ThinkReset, a text-space instantiation of this view. ThinkReset explicitly constructs reusable intermediate interfaces through interface writeback and reset, and directly optimizes post-reset continuation success. Across multiple long-horizon reasoning benchmarks, this perspective consistently improves success rates under fixed context windows.

</details>


### [80] [TAPR: Enhancing LLM Performance with a Task-Aware Prompt Rewriter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28657)
*Oliver Savolainen,Emanuele Bastianelli,Hosein Azarbonyad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TAPR（Task-Aware Prompt Rewriter），一个通过强化学习（GRPO）训练的提示词重写模型，旨在将用户输入改写为任务优化的提示词，从而提升下游LLM的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM通常需要精心设计的提示词才能发挥全部潜力，这对非专业用户构成了使用障碍。现有方法缺乏一种自动化的、面向特定任务的提示词优化机制，因此需要一个能够将用户提示词改写成更优形式以提升下游模型表现的方案。

Method: 采用强化学习框架中的Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）训练TAPR模型，奖励信号来源于LLM-as-judge对改写后提示词及对应任务输出的双重评估。以Phi-4-mini-instruct作为基础模型进行微调，使TAPR具备提示词重写能力。

Result: 在问题回答、摘要生成和算术推理等多种任务上进行实验，TAPR在提示词改写能力上相比基础模型取得一致提升。在Natural Questions和GSM8K等基准上，TAPR生成的提示词包含更清晰、更具指导性的语言，从而获得更高的准确率。

Conclusion: TAPR验证了通过强化学习训练提示词改写器的有效性，能够自动生成更清晰、更具任务针对性的提示词，降低LLM使用门槛，并已在多个基准上证实性能提升。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often require carefully crafted prompts to unlock their full potential, which can be a barrier for non-expert users. This work addresses the challenge by introducing a Task-Aware Prompt Rewriter (TAPR), a model that reformulates user prompts into task-optimized prompts with the explicit goal of improving downstream LLM performance. We train TAPR using reinforcement learning with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), where rewards are derived from LLM-as-judge evaluations of both the reformulated prompt and the corresponding task output. Experimental results on diverse tasks, such as question answering, summarization, and arithmetic reasoning, show that our method yields consistent gains over base models in prompt rewriting ability. Fine-tuning Phi-4-mini-instruct (as the base model for TAPR) produces prompts that contain clearer and more instructive language, leading to higher accuracy on established benchmarks such as Natural Questions and GSM8K. Our code is available at: https://github.com/OliverSavolainen/task-specific-prompt-rewriter

</details>


### [81] [Empowering Cross-Domain Sequential Recommendation with Hybrid Tokenization and Serial-Parallel Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28659)
*Yuxuan Hu,Yuhao Wang,Tianbo Huang,Chao Zhang,Ziwei Liu,Lihua Zhang,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GenCDSR是一个面向跨域序列推荐（CDSR）的生成式推荐框架，通过跨域混合分词机制和跨域串并行解码策略，在提升推荐准确率的同时显著降低推理延迟。实验显示相比最先进基线，准确率平均提升1.5%，推理延迟平均降低85.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域序列推荐（CDSR）方法存在两个关键缺陷：(1) 在分词过程中忽略了跨域协作相关性；(2) 采用波束搜索等低效解码策略，推理耗时严重，阻碍了实时部署。

Method: 提出GenCDSR框架，包含两部分核心设计：(1) 跨域混合分词机制，采用多塔架构，通过层级共享-特异编码本（hierarchical shared-specific codebooks）和细粒度编码本联合捕获跨域共性与域间差异；(2) 跨域串并行解码策略，利用层级语义标识（SID）结构实现部分的并行化生成，在保持生成一致性的同时大幅降低推理延迟。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上的实验表明，GenCDSR相比最先进的基线方法，平均准确率提升1.5%，平均推理延迟降低85.1%。代码和数据集已开源。

Conclusion: GenCDSR证明了通过合理的跨域分词设计和层级结构感知的并行解码策略，可以有效解决生成式推荐在跨域场景下的性能与效率权衡问题，为CDSR的实时部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Cross-domain sequential recommendation (CDSR) aims to model users' dynamic interest transitions and sequential patterns across multiple domains. Recently, generative recommendation (GR) has emerged. It first learns semantic identifiers (SIDs) from item semantics and formulates recommendation as autoregressive generation. However, existing methods face two critical issues: (1) they ignore collaborative correlations across domains during tokenization, and (2) they adopt inefficient decoding strategies, such as beam search, during generation, which hinders real-time deployment. To address these limitations, we propose GenCDSR, an effective and efficient generative framework for CDSR. Specifically, we design a cross-domain hybrid tokenization mechanism with a multi-tower architecture to jointly capture cross-domain commonalities and domain-specific distinctions through hierarchical shared-specific and fine-grained codebooks. Furthermore, we develop a cross-domain serial-parallel decoding strategy that leverages the hierarchical SID structure to partially parallelize generation, significantly reducing inference latency while preserving generation consistency. Experiments on three public datasets show that GenCDSR achieves an average accuracy improvement of 1.5 percent and an average inference latency reduction of 85.1 percent compared with state-of-the-art baselines. The implementation code and datasets are available online: https://github.com/Applied-Machine-Learning-Lab/RecSys2026_GenCDSR.

</details>


### [82] [An Ontology-Guided, Deduplication-Aware Extraction Layer for Knowledge Graph Construction from Heterogeneous Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28662)
*Vaibhav Dangaich,Kevin Lewis,Kundeshwar Pundalik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并实现了一个生产级知识图谱抽取系统，通过本体引导的两阶段抽取和五阶段精炼管线，将非结构化文档流转化为符合正式本体的验证知识图谱。系统采用本地部署的Qwen3.5-9B模型，通过嵌入相似度从图数据库在线检索本体相关切片注入提示词，将目录开销降低约94%，并将搜索召回率从约70%提升至95%且无误合并，同时修复了七类静默质量缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在从非结构化文档中抽取实体和关系时存在显著一致性问题：类型词汇表在文档间割裂、同一人物以多种姓名变体出现、关系重复、同名个体可能被静默合并。这些缺陷严重损害了知识图谱的可靠性和可用性。

Method: 系统设计包含三个核心部分：(1) 消费Kafka文档元数据，通过针对PDF、电子表格、Office和图像格式的处理器路由内容；(2) 使用本地托管的Qwen3.5-9B模型（针对本体微调）进行两遍抽取；关键在于本体引导抽取——通过嵌入相似度从图数据库实时检索精选本体的相关切片注入抽取提示词；(3) 五阶段精炼管线：确定性清洗、跨块合并、关系第二遍抽取、六种无需模型推理的去重算法，以及带冲突保护的嵌入解析子系统（任何相似度分数均不能覆盖）。

Result: 在情报语料上的评估显示：搜索召回率从约70%提升至95%且无误合并；目录开销相比静态领域切片降低约94%；修复了七类静默质量缺陷，包括截断源文本单个字符的bug，以及携带头衔前缀实体的系统性重复问题。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了"本体引导抽取+确定性后处理精炼"这一混合架构在生产环境中可有效解决LLM抽取的一致性问题，在降低推理开销（通过本体切片检索）的同时显著提升知识图谱质量，为大规模文档流的知识抽取提供了一套可落地的工程范式。

Abstract: Large language models extract entities and relationships from unstructured documents fluently but inconsistently: type vocabularies fracture across documents, the same person surfaces under several name variants, relationships duplicate, and distinct individuals who share a name risk silent conflation. This paper presents the design, implementation, and empirical refinement of a production extraction layer that converts a live document stream into a validated knowledge graph aligned to a formal ontology. The system consumes document metadata from Kafka, routes PDF, spreadsheet, Office, and image content through handlers built for each format, and extracts entities and relationships in two passes using a locally hosted Qwen3.5-9B model tuned on the ontology. Its distinguishing component is ontology-guided extraction: the relevant slice of a curated ontology is retrieved live from a graph database by embedding similarity and injected into the extraction prompt, reducing catalog overhead by about 94 percent relative to static domain slices. Extracted results then pass through a refinement pipeline of five stages: deterministic cleaning, merging across chunks, a second pass for relationships, six deduplication algorithms that require no model inference, and an embedding resolution subsystem whose conflict guard no similarity score can override. Evaluation on intelligence corpora improved search recall from roughly 70 to 95 percent with no false merges, and corrected seven classes of silent quality defect, ranging from a bug that truncated source text by a single character to the systematic duplication of entities that carried title prefixes.

</details>


### [83] [How Hard Does It Think? Analyzing Step-Aware Reasoning Energy in LLM Chain-of-Thought Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28674)
*Hui Wei,Junda Wu,Sheldon Yu,Sizhe Zhou,Yizhu Jiao,Ming Zhong,Bowen Jin,Tong Yu,Shijia Pan,Jiawei Han,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SARE（Step-Aware Reasoning Energy）框架，通过Centered Kernel Alignment（CKA）度量相邻Transformer层间token隐藏状态的Gram矩阵，实现链式思维推理中单个步骤级别的计算努力量化，并在六个推理基准和三个开源LLM上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可解释性方法要么依赖输出层信号，要么将处理深度压缩成单一轨迹级标量，无法揭示链式思维推理中各步骤之间计算努力分配的内部机制，步骤级别的努力分配仍然是开放性问题。

Method: SARE通过CKA计算相邻Transformer层间token隐藏状态Gram矩阵的相似度来量化单个推理步骤的能量，无需特征向量对齐或聚类对应。此外，将推理轨迹建模为潜在语义状态之间的转移，将推理能量置于语义进展的大背景下进行情境化分析。

Result: 在六个推理基准和三个开源LLM上的实验表明：推理能量在不同步骤类型间高度非均匀，存在轨迹级指标无法观察到的类相位转变；错误轨迹在关键推理节点上表现出系统性较低的能量；基于SARE的特征在大多数设置中匹配或超过基于输出置信度的基线。

Conclusion: 内部几何动态编码了超越表面级信号的预测信息，SARE能够揭示链式思维推理中步骤级别的计算努力分配规律，为推理过程的细粒度可解释性提供了新的几何框架。

Abstract: Understanding how computational effort is allocated across individual chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning steps remains an open challenge: existing interpretability methods rely on output-level signals or collapse processing depth into a single trajectory-level scalar, leaving step-wise effort opaque. We propose Step-Aware Reasoning Energy (SARE), a geometric framework that quantifies effort at the granularity of individual CoT steps via Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) between Gram matrices of token hidden states across adjacent transformer layers, capturing inter-token relational structure without requiring eigenvector alignment or cluster correspondence. SARE further contextualizes this energy within reasoning's semantic progression by modeling CoT trajectories as transitions among latent semantic states. Across six reasoning benchmarks and three open-weight LLMs, we find that reasoning energy is highly non-uniform across step types, exhibiting phase-like transitions invisible to trajectory-level metrics; incorrect trajectories show systematically lower energy at critical reasoning junctions; and SARE-based features match or outperform output-based confidence baselines in most settings, indicating that internal geometric dynamics encode predictive information beyond surface-level signals.

</details>


### [84] [Reasoning in Real World Clinical Care: Why Large Language Models Are Not Yet Safe for Autonomous Clinical Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28677)
*Shayndhan Sivanathan,Shravan Nageswaran,Mehdi Zadem,Ryaan Sultan,Nicolas von Mallinckrodt,Max Solovyev,Alexey Matyushkin,Sumon Sadhu,Gabriele C DeLuca,Sanjeeva Jeyaretna,James Hillis,Manoj Ramachandran,Prakash Jayakumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇Perspective观点文章，聚焦LLM在自主分诊（autonomous triage）应用中的安全性问题。作者指出，尽管LLM在医疗执照考试和诊断推理上表现优异，但将LLM用于对未分化患者进行自主分诊（几乎无临床医生介入）时，安全性证据尚不存在。核心缺陷不在于医学知识，而在于临床评估的保真度：LLM优化目标是生成最可能的文本，而非在不对称代价下做出安全决策。安全分诊需要的是在不确定条件下主动收集信息、扩大鉴别诊断、寻找危险信号、降低升级阈值等能力，而这些行为恰恰是评估中容易掩盖的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已通过医疗执照考试，且在精选案例中可与医生媲美，推动了其在症状评估、临床决策支持、行政文档和基于规则的警报增强中的广泛应用。其中最危险的用途是将LLM用于几乎无临床医生介入的自主分诊。作者写作动机是警示：这一应用方向缺乏安全性证据，且当前评估方法的局限性可能掩盖实质性风险。

Method: 本文为Perspective（观点性文章），通过逻辑论证和概念分析来说明问题。作者对比了文本预测优化与安全医疗决策所需的优化目标差异，指出安全分诊本质上是"不对称代价下的序贯决策"（sequential decision under asymmetric cost），而非简单选择最可能的诊断。作者归纳了LLM在信息不完整情况下可能缺失的关键行为（扩大鉴别诊断、寻找遗漏的危险信号、降低升级阈值、推迟判断、在排除高危害诊断前保持警觉），并分析了评估方法的不足（使用完整、精选、置信门控的模拟数据）以及LLM的辅助型行为偏差（轻信、顺从、校准不良）如何放大风险。

Result: 文章得出核心结论：LLM在自主分诊任务上的安全性尚无可信证据。主要问题包括：(1) 优化目标不匹配——最大化文本概率不等于最大化安全决策；(2) 在信息收集（information gathering）这一核心临床技能上存在结构性缺陷；(3) 现有评估方法（依赖完整、精选的病例模拟）无法暴露真实世界中的失败模式；(4) 缺乏临床分诊逻辑约束时，LLM的顺从性、轻信性和校准不良等行为偏差会进一步放大安全风险。

Conclusion: 作者认为，安全分诊的关键不是选择最可能的诊断，而是在不对称代价下做出序贯决策——一次灾难性的漏诊远超多次误报的代价，而决定性信号可能来自患者未主动提供、模型也未经过训练去主动获取的信息。因此，LLM应用于自主分诊前，必须解决信息收集的不确定性管理能力、行为偏差约束以及评估方法学（需在真实、信息不完整条件下测试）等根本性问题。

Abstract: LLM now pass medical licensing examinations and, in curated cases, can rival physicians at diagnostic reasoning. These developments have accelerated the use of LLMs for symptom assessment and clinical decision support in diagnostic and treatment guidance, administrative documentation, and rules-based alert enhancement. This Perspective concerns the most consequential of these applications: the autonomous triage of self-presenting, undifferentiated patients, with little or no clinician in the loop. For that task, the evidence of safety does not yet exist. The gap is not in medical knowledge but in the fidelity of clinical evaluation: a model optimized to continue the most probable text is not optimized to act safely when the safe answer is the improbable must-not-miss diagnosis. Safe triage is not the selection of the most likely diagnosis; it is a sequential decision under asymmetric cost, in which the single catastrophic miss outweighs many false alarms, and the decisive signal may be one the patient has not volunteered - and that the model has not been trained to seek. The core deficit is therefore one of information gathering under uncertainty. Under incomplete histories, LLM systems may fail to show the behaviors safe triage requires: broadening the differential; seeking the missing red flag; lowering the threshold for escalation; deferring judgement until sufficient information is obtained; and escalating concern where high-harm diagnoses remain unexcluded. These modes of failure for LLMs can be difficult to detect considering that evaluations to date often use complete, well-curated, confidence-gated simulations. The application of LLMs under these conditions may be amplified by assistant-like behaviors and positive bias, including credulity, agreeableness, and miscalibration - when these are not constrained by clinical triage logic.

</details>


### [85] [ViSAGE: Constructing Self-Correcting Memories for Long-Form Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28678)
*Xinkui Zhao,Enbo Chen,Yifan Zhang,Chang Liu,Guanjie Cheng,Naibo Wang,Yueshen Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ViSAGE是一个多模态智能体记忆框架，通过跨模态绑定锚定实体身份、双向记忆精炼传播延迟的身份证据，并引入多智能体交叉验证实现身份-证据对齐约束下的证据评估与弃权机制，在长时程多模态任务上超出最强基线5.9%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆方法在激进压缩和分段处理下丢失细粒度身份线索，且过度依赖向量相似度检索，可能返回语义相关但身份不匹配的证据，导致实体混淆、错误传播和幻觉回答。

Method: ViSAGE包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于跨模态绑定的实体身份锚定，在长时间范围内保持身份一致性；(2) 双向记忆精炼，传播延迟的身份证据，回溯性统一历史记录并改进未来推理；(3) 多智能体交叉验证，在身份-证据对齐约束下评估检索证据，并在证据缺失时选择弃权而非生成无依据的回答。

Result: 大量实验表明ViSAGE在长时程多模态智能体任务上持续优于最强基线，准确率提升5.9%。

Conclusion: ViSAGE通过构建自纠正的实体中心记忆，有效解决了多模态长时程推理中的身份混淆问题，并在证据不足时通过弃权机制减少幻觉，展示了实体一致性记忆对多模态智能体长期推理的重要性。

Abstract: Multimodal agents operating in long-horizon environments must build and continually update multimedia memories to support entity-consistent, temporally grounded reasoning. However, existing agentic memory approaches often discard fine-grained dentity cues under aggressive compression and segment-wise processing. They also rely heavily on vector similarity retrieval, which can surface semantically related yet identity-mismatched evidence, leading to entity confusion, error propagation, and hallucinated answers.
  We propose ViSAGE, a multimodal agentic memory framework that constructs self-correcting, entity-centric memories. Specifically, ViSAGE anchors entity identity via cross-modal binding over long temporal ranges. It then applies bidirectional memory refinement to propagate delayed identity evidence, retroactively unifying historical records and improving future reasoning. We also introduce multi-agent cross-verification to assess retrieved evidence under an identity-evidence alignment onstraint, enabling abstention instead of unsupported answers when evidence is missing. Extensive results demonstrate that ViSAGE consistently outperforms the strongest baseline, achieving 5.9% higher accuracy.

</details>


### [86] [Multi-Agent Planning with Spatio-Temporal and Topological Constraints using STL-GO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28679)
*Sheryl Paul,Vidisha Kudalkar,Anand Balakrishnan,Lars Lindemann,Alberto Speranzon,Jyotirmoy V. Deshmukh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了满足STL-GO（带图算子的时空逻辑）约束的多智能体路径规划问题，提出了基于混合整数规划（MIP）和可满足性模理论（SMT）的两种编码方法，并提供了统一接口供用户指定智能体约束、图拓扑和STL-GO规范。在多无人机搜索救援基准上进行了评估，验证了两种编码在动态多图交互下的表达能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体规划问题在工程应用中广泛存在（如多机器人灭火、工厂无人机巡检），其核心挑战在于时空约束（智能体何时何地做什么）和拓扑约束（智能体间如何交互）的同时满足。尽管已有多种时空逻辑框架被提出，但STL-GO作为一个能表达多智能体及其拓扑（感知、通信、任务）关系的近期形式化工具，其规划问题尚未得到充分解决，尤其是需要编码多个潜在时变图结构的这一难题。

Method: 本文提出两种将STL-GO规划问题形式化的编码方法：（1）基于混合整数规划（MIP）的编码；（2）基于可满足性模理论（SMT）的编码。两种编码均提供可靠性保证。同时设计了一个统一接口，用于指定智能体约束、图拓扑及STL-GO规范，使得两种方法可以无缝切换和直接对比。

Result: 在多无人机搜索救援基准实验中，作者对智能体团队规模和图的复杂度进行了消融分析，结果表明两种编码在动态多图交互场景下均能有效求解STL-GO规划问题，充分体现了所提编码的表达能力。

Conclusion: 本文通过MIP和SMT两种编码路径解决了STL-GO约束下的多智能体路径规划问题，为时空-拓扑联合约束的规划提供了可计算且可比较的解决方案框架，并为后续在该方向上的进一步研究（如优化、分布式扩展）奠定了基础。

Abstract: Multi-agent planning problems arise in a variety of engineering applications, such as multi-robot wildfire fighting and unmanned aerial inspection in factories. A particular challenge is the existence of spatio-temporal (i.e., when and/or where an agent should do what) and topological constraints (i.e., how agents should interact), as typically formalized via the notion of graphs. Over the last years, various frameworks have been proposed that can capture such constraints via spatio-temporal logics. We focus here on spatio-temporal logic with graph operators (STL-GO), a recent formalism that supports reasoning about multiple agents and their topologies, such as sensing, communication, and task topologies. In this paper, we consider the problem of planning multi-agent paths that satisfy constraints written in STL-GO. This problem is particularly challenging due to the need of encoding multiple, potentially time-varying graphs via the graph operators inherent to STL-GO. We present two encodings of this problem, one based on mixed-integer programming (MIP) and another based on satisfiability modulo theory (SMT), with soundness guarantees. We provide a unified interface for specifying agent constraints, their graph topologies, and the STL-GO specification, enabling seamless use of both methods and facilitating direct comparison between them. We evaluate both encodings on a multi-UAV search-and-rescue benchmark, ablating over team size and graph complexity, highlighting the expressiveness of the proposed encodings under dynamic multi- graph interactions.

</details>


### [87] [Library Reachability in LSR-Synth: How Anti-Memorization Design Changes the Measurement of Symbolic Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28684)
*Zhan'ao Yao,Liang Yin,Zhihao Gao,Boxuan Zhang,Xiaoyu Wu,Linjing Li,Rongyan Wang,Tingwei Chen,Youwei Wang,Xiaolin Zhao,Jiahui Shi,Jianjun Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对LSR-Synth基准进行严格测量评估，探究其任务能否区分语言模型提供的科学先验与传统不考虑任务语义的运算符搜索。研究发现固定词汇表已覆盖大部分任务，语言模型生成候选的边际贡献有限，仅在选择性破坏词汇覆盖时才显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学方程发现基准多由公开已知方程构成，难以区分模型是真正从数据中发现规律还是从训练语料中回忆答案。LSR-Synth通过引入新颖合成项来解决此问题，但需要进一步验证这些任务能否分辨语言模型的科学先验与不考虑任务语义的常规运算符搜索。

Method: 构建一个使用固定词汇表且具有公开来源记录的无语义基线；通过语义盲化、词典削弱和匹配的算子族剔除三种方法来评估候选覆盖率的作用；并在严格分布外（OOD）评估下检验结果的稳健性。

Result: 在当前任务快照、搜索预算和评分协议下，固定词汇表已覆盖大多数任务；语言模型生成的候选很少扩展可解实例集合，其边际贡献只有在词汇覆盖率被选择性破坏时才变得显著。严格OOD评估降低了所有方法的绝对成功率，但未改变这一关系。

Conclusion: 现有大部分LSR-Synth任务适合评估对未见表达式的拟合与重组能力，但不足以单独识别超出固定搜索空间的先验贡献。该发现既不否定LSR-Synth对完整公式记忆的控制，也不意味着语言模型先验普遍无用，而是支持一个更有限的结论。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks for scientific equation discovery are largely composed of well-known equations available in the public domain, making it difficult to determine whether a model is discovering laws from data or merely recalling answers from its training corpus. LSR-Synth mitigates this problem by introducing novel synthetic terms into established scientific mechanisms and filtering the resulting tasks for novelty, solvability, and scientific plausibility. This paper examines a narrower measurement question: can these tasks further distinguish scientific priors supplied by language models from conventional operator search that does not access task semantics? We construct a semantics-free baseline using a fixed vocabulary with publicly documented provenance, and assess the role of candidate coverage through semantic blinding, library weakening, and matched operator-family knockouts. Under the current task snapshot, search budget, and scoring protocol, the fixed vocabulary already covers most tasks, while language-model-generated candidates rarely expand the set of solvable instances. Their marginal contribution becomes substantial only when vocabulary coverage is selectively disrupted. Strict out-of-distribution evaluation lowers the absolute success rates of all methods but does not alter this relationship. These findings neither invalidate LSR-Synth's controls against memorization of complete formulas nor imply that language-model priors are generally unhelpful. Rather, they support a more limited conclusion: most current tasks remain suitable for evaluating the fitting and recombination of previously unseen expressions, but are insufficient on their own to identify contributions from priors beyond a fixed search space.

</details>


### [88] [Safety, or Just Capability? A Validity Audit of Agent-Safety Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28685)
*Youting Wang,Xiao Han,Dingyan Shang,Yuan Tang,Bowen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统验证了四个智能体安全基准（R-Judge、InjecAgent、AgentHarm、AgentDojo）作为测量工具的有效性，发现这些基准存在严重问题：二元轨迹判断基准（R-Judge）在F1评分下存在"恒阳性"策略即可获得高分的缺陷；各基准对同一批模型的排名不一致且分歧源于小样本面板伪迹；能力与不同安全目标的关联方向截然相反（对任务成功正相关、对越位安全负相关），且在扩展面板上结论不稳健。作者呼吁安全声明必须明确基准、指标、目标行为和模型面板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同智能体安全基准衡量不同的行为，但其分数却被随意互换引用为智能体的安全水平。这种"以偏概全"式的引用方式缺乏科学依据，本文旨在通过系统验证这些基准作为测量工具的有效性，揭示其作为科学测量工具是否经得起检验。

Method: 作者将四个基准（R-Judge、InjecAgent、AgentHarm、AgentDojo）视为待验证的测量工具，在其官方实现和作者提供的评分器下对多达22个模型进行运行；同时用一个统一协议测量MMLU和GPQA作为能力合成指标。通过分析F1指标缺陷、基准间排名分歧的小样本面板伪迹、能力与安全关联的方向性差异，以及扩展面板（41模型）上的稳健性检验（留一组织、组织聚类bootstrap）来评估各基准的结构效度。

Result: 主要发现包括：(1) R-Judge上"恒阳性"策略F1达0.690，高于21个实际区分模型中5个；(2) 三个广覆盖基准对18个模型排名不一致，分歧源于小样本伪迹（R-Judge与AgentHarm的相关系数在n=7时为-0.64，在n=18时变为+0.02）；(3) 能力对任务成功正相关（ρ=+0.60），对越位安全负相关（ρ=-0.44），在21模型和20配对面板上均显著；(4) 扩展至41模型后两种关联均趋弱且不再显著；(5) AgentHarm与能力控制后的三模板越位安全强相关（ρ=+0.72），但这仅是聚合效度而非通用安全证据。

Conclusion: 现有智能体安全基准作为测量工具存在系统性缺陷：指标层面有恒阳性策略漏洞，基准间缺乏趋同效度，样本面板规模对结论稳定性影响巨大。安全声明必须至少明确基准、指标、目标行为和模型面板四要素，否则无法称其为严谨的安全测量。

Abstract: Agent-safety benchmarks measure different behaviors, and their scores get quoted interchangeably as an agent's safety. We treat four of them (R-Judge, InjecAgent, AgentHarm, AgentDojo) as measurements to be validated, running each under its official implementation and author-provided scorer on up to 22 models, with MMLU and GPQA measured by us under one protocol as a capability composite. The metric is the first problem. On any binary trace-judgment benchmark scored by $F_1$, an ``always positive'' policy attains $F_1 = 2π/(1+π)$; on R-Judge that is $0.690$, above five of the 21 models that actually discriminate. The three broad-coverage benchmarks then rank the same 18 models differently, and the trade-off behind that disagreement is a small-panel artifact: R-Judge specificity against AgentHarm safety correlates $-0.64$ at $n{=}7$ and $+0.02$ at $n{=}18$, and a quarter of random size-7 subsets reach $|ρ| \geq 0.5$ around that near-zero value. Held-out validity turns on which outcome you pick. Capability predicts task success ($ρ{=}{+}0.60$) but correlates negatively with misalignment safety ($ρ{=}{-}0.44$, $n{=}21$). On their paired $n{=}20$ panel, the corresponding contrast is $Δ{=}{-}1.00$ (95% CI $[-1.48, -0.49]$, $p<0.001$), and it survives leave-one-organization-out and organization-clustered bootstrap analyses. On an expanded 41-model panel, the misalignment correlation weakens to $-0.16$ (95% CI $[-0.54, +0.22]$) and jailbreak strengthens to $+0.34$, though neither change is significant. \mbox{AgentHarm} shows the strongest held-out association, $ρ{=}{+}0.72$ with three-template jailbreak safety after controlling capability. But both instruments score harmful compliance, so this is evidence of convergent validity rather than general safety. Naming the benchmark, metric, target behavior, and model panel is the minimum a safety claim needs.

</details>


### [89] [SciToolAgent-Evo: An Ontology-Aware Self-Evolving Agent for Open-World Scientific Tool Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28692)
*Yuqi Tang,Chenyi Zhou,Libin Wang,Keyan Ding,Qiang Zhang,Huajun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SciToolAgent-Evo，一个本体感知的自进化智能体，用于开放世界科学工具的获取。它通过技能/经验记忆和本体化工具图驱动，在推理时使用LinUCB赌臂门控动态平衡探索与利用，并在线补全新工具的科学本体。作者还构建了包含900个任务和四个难度等级的OpenSciToolBench基准，实验表明该方法达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体依赖预定义工具空间和静态语义，无法适应开放世界科学工作流中工具需求、能力和边界动态演变的场景。需要一个能够自主获取和整合新工具的智能体。

Method: 1) 采用持续演化的记忆机制存储技能与经验；2) 构建本体化的工具图（ontologized tool graph）；3) 从对比轨迹中蒸馏可泛化知识（accumulation阶段）；4) 推理阶段主动提出工具请求，利用基于LinUCB的赌臂门控动态平衡探索与利用；5) 新工具获取后通过在线本体补全无缝集成到已有知识图中。

Result: 在自建基准OpenSciToolBench（900个真实任务，4个难度级别）上，SciToolAgent-Evo取得了最先进的性能，验证了其鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: SciToolAgent-Evo有效解决了开放世界科学工作流中工具动态演化的挑战，通过自进化机制和在线本体集成实现了新工具的自主获取与无缝整合，为LLM智能体在开放科学场景中的应用提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have been increasingly adopted in scientific research for organizing and invoking specialized computational tools. However, their reliance on predefined tool spaces with static semantics limits their applicability to open-world scientific workflows, where tool requirements, capabilities, and boundaries evolve dynamically. To this end, we propose SciToolAgent-Evo, an ontology-aware self-evolving agent for open-world scientific tool acquisition. Driven by an evolving memory of skills, experiences, and an ontologized tool graph, it distills generalizable knowledge from contrastive trajectories during accumulation, whereas during inference, it formulates active requests and utilizes a LinUCB-based bandit gate to dynamically balance exploration and exploitation. Once a novel tool is acquired, its scientific ontology is completed online for seamless integration into the known graph. Moreover, we introduce OpenSciToolBench, a benchmark containing 900 realistic tasks across four difficulty levels. Extensive evaluations show that SciToolAgent-Evo achieves state-of-the-art performance, validating its robustness and generalization.

</details>


### [90] [EarlyDx: An Admission-Anchored Benchmark for Open-Ended Generation of Evidence-Supported ED-Encounter Diagnoses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28788)
*Jiahui Li,Ruili Fang,Zishuai Liu,Yutong Guo,Nan Yang,Wenzhan Song,Jin Lu,Fei Dou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EarlyDx，一个基于MIMIC-IV中154,834次急诊科就诊记录的大规模开放早期诊断基准。与现有基准不同，EarlyDx限制在入院时t0可用的记录，使用急诊就诊期间记录的诊断而非出院诊断进行监督，并通过LLM审计员验证每个自由文本标签的证据支持程度。评估发现，包括前沿通用模型、医学专用模型和领域内后训练模型在内的所有系统都无法可靠地综合入院时证据，零样本模型仅恢复3-31%需要推断的诊断，后训练将推断相关召回率提升至56%但仍存在较大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有诊断预测基准不适合临床入院诊断场景：它们限制在封闭代码集、排除自由文本笔记、并使用包含完整住院过程的出院诊断进行监督。这促使研究者构建一个更贴近真实急诊临床决策场景的基准。

Method: 从MIMIC-IV中提取154,834次急诊科就诊，将证据限制在入院时刻t0可获得的记录，使用急诊就诊期间记录的诊断而非出院诊断作为标签。采用LLM审计员对每个自由文本标签进行证据支持度验证（支持/部分支持/不支持），主评估仅评分完全支持的标签。采用语义LLM-as-judge协议进行评估。

Result: 零样本模型主要依赖提取而非推断，仅能恢复3-31%需要推断的诊断；后训练可将推断依赖的召回率提升至56%，但仍存在显著差距；在时间关键性条件下，没有任何系统达到临床医生的敏感性与精确性平衡。

Conclusion: 当前系统（包括前沿通用模型、医学专用模型和领域内后训练模型）均无法可靠地综合入院时证据进行早期诊断推断，后训练虽有提升但差距依然显著，表明早期诊断推断任务仍是未解决的挑战。

Abstract: Clinical diagnosis at hospital admission must be made rapidly from limited, incomplete evidence. Existing diagnosis-prediction benchmarks are poorly suited to this setting: they restrict prediction to closed code sets, exclude free-text notes, and supervise with discharge diagnoses that incorporate the full inpatient course. We introduce EarlyDx, a large-scale benchmark for open-ended early diagnosis, built from 154,834 emergency department encounters in MIMIC-IV. Each encounter is restricted to records available at admission time $t_0$ and supervised by the diagnoses recorded during the ED encounter rather than at discharge. An LLM auditor further verifies every free-text label as supported, partially supported, or unsupported by that evidence; the primary evaluation scores only fully supported labels. Under a semantic LLM-as-judge protocol, no evaluated system --- frontier general, medical-specialized, or in-domain post-trained --- synthesizes admission-time evidence reliably. Zero-shot models score largely by extraction, recovering only 3-31% of diagnoses that must be inferred rather than read from the record; post-training raises inference-dependent recall to 56%, but a sizeable margin remains, and on time-critical conditions no system attains a clinician's balance of sensitivity and precision. We release the full construction and evaluation pipeline at here.

</details>


### [91] [Model or Harness? An Interaction-Centric Taxonomy for Localizing Agent Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28802)
*Harsh Raj,Vipul Gupta,Anas Mahmoud,Razvan-Gabriel Dumitru,Darvin Yi,Aakash Sabharwal,Yunzhong He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以交互为中心的智能体失败分类法，通过将41种失败模式分配到组件间的边和故障侧，将失败定位到具体交互环节并明确修复归属，解决了智能体系统评估中"修复分配问题"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估将智能体失败简化为系统级结果，掩盖了失败源头和应采取的干预措施，导致"修复分配问题"——相同的外部失败可能源于模型、脚手架、环境或基准的不同缺陷，而现有失败分类法多为基准特化、缺乏共享结构，难以指导改进。

Method: 提出基于交互的分类法，将41种失败模式组织为组件对之间的边，并为每条边标注"故障侧"以指示修复归属；通过公开基准、系统卡片、已发表报告和日志轨迹中的工作示例进行验证，并使用四个前沿模型作为独立推理评判者评估分类法的可复现性。

Result: 最强评判者在四个前沿模型中对人类类别标签达到Cohen's κ=0.76，表明该分类法捕捉的是共享结构而非标注者的特化偏好，并适用于从编码助手到长时程个人助手和多智能体系统等多种架构。

Conclusion: 该分类法使失败分类具有可操作性：模型侧失败指向后训练目标，脚手架侧失败指向工具集成修复，环境或评分器失败则暴露需要重新设计的评测条件。

Abstract: Existing evaluations often reduce agent failures to system-level outcomes, obscuring where the fault originated and which intervention would improve the agent system. This creates a repair-assignment problem: the same visible failure may call for model post-training, harness engineering, environment redesign, or benchmark repair depending on its source. Because agent behavior emerges from interactions among models, harnesses, users, tools, memory, and environments, outcome-level labels are often insufficient for improvement. Most failure taxonomies do little to resolve this problem because they are benchmark-specific and lack a shared structure. We introduce an interaction-centric taxonomy that localizes failures to the interactions in which they originate and identifies the responsible component. It organizes 41 failure modes by assigning each to an edge between two components and a fault side indicating where the repair belongs. This makes the taxonomy actionable: model-side failures identify targets for post-training, harness-side failures point to scaffolding and tool-integration fixes, and environment or grader failures reveal evaluation conditions requiring redesign. The schema applies across agent architectures, from coding assistants to long-horizon personal assistants and multi-agent systems. We ground the taxonomy in worked examples from public benchmarks, model system cards, published reports, and logged agent trajectories, and evaluate its reproducibility using independent reasoning agents as judges. Across four frontier models, the strongest judge reaches Cohen's $κ=0.76$ against human category labels, suggesting that the categories capture shared structure rather than annotator-specific preferences.

</details>


### [92] [Best Friends, Not Forever: Evaluating Long-Horizon Persona Collapse and Behavioral Drift in AI Companions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28818)
*Pranav Narayanan Venkit,Akshara Prabhakar,Yu Li,Daniel Lee,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究AI陪伴系统在长时程互动中的两个可观测故障——"人格坍缩"（persona collapse，失去已部署的角色、边界、价值观或风格）和"行为漂移"（behavioral drift，这些特性的逐渐或反复侵蚀）。作者提出ANCHOR受控合成审计框架，分别测量人格执行与轨迹回忆，包含2,008段对话、27种人格、9种交互日程、3种生成记忆设置、4个评估模型。结果显示没有一个模型与配置能可靠保持任一维度：轨迹准确率仅44.4%，用户状态回忆接近四选项随机水平，且条件/记忆无法解决这些失效。问卷保留率因模型与人格维度而异，与逐轮行为不一致，且受评估者选择影响，表明当前系统无法可靠支持长时程陪伴连续性，审计需区分人格执行、轨迹回忆、评估者来源与部署语境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI陪伴系统越来越多地介入重复性社交互动，用户依赖于稳定的角色设定与共享历史，但"局部可接受"的回复并不保证这两者能够持续存在。已有工作缺乏对长时程连续性故障的系统化、可分离测量，作者因此希望明确刻画并量化人格坍缩与行为漂移这两种可观察的失效模式。

Method: 提出ANCHOR受控合成审计框架：(1) Identity Probe结合102项密封问卷与逐轮判断来测量人格执行；(2) Trajectory Probe从35个对话库中构建110个校准的反事实问题来测量轨迹回忆。实验覆盖27种人格、9种交互日程、3种生成记忆设置、4个评估模型，共2,008段对话。

Result: 轨迹平均准确率仅44.4%，用户状态回忆接近四选项随机水平；没有任何上下文条件或记忆机制能稳定解决这些失效。问卷保留率随模型与人格维度变化，与逐轮行为测量不一致，且对评估者选择敏感。

Conclusion: 当前系统尚不能可靠地支持长时程陪伴连续性。审计必须将人格执行、轨迹回忆、评估者来源与部署语境作为独立维度分别测量，而不应将其合并为单一的"可信度"或"稳定性"分数。

Abstract: As AI companions increasingly mediate repeated social interaction, users may rely on a stable role and shared history, yet locally acceptable replies do not ensure that either persists. We study two observable long-horizon failures: 'persona collapse', the loss of a deployed role, boundaries, values, or style, and 'behavioral drift', the gradual or recurrent erosion of those properties. We introduce ANCHOR, a controlled synthetic audit that separately measures persona enactment and trajectory recall. The study contains 2,008 conversations spanning 27 personas, nine interaction schedules, three generated memory settings, and four evaluated models. The Identity Probe combines a sealed 102-item questionnaire with turn-level judgments, while the Trajectory Probe scores 110 calibrated counterfactual questions from 35 conversation banks. Our results show that no evaluated model and configuration reliably preserves either dimensions: trajectory accuracy averages only 44.4%, user-state recall remains near four-option chance, and no tested context condition or memory consistently resolves these failures. Questionnaire retention also varies by model and persona facet, disagrees with turn-level behavior, and is sensitive to evaluator choice. These results indicate that current systems do not yet reliably support long-horizon companion continuity and that audits must distinguish persona enactment, trajectory recall, evaluator provenance, and deployment context rather than collapse them into a single trust or stability score.

</details>


### [93] [Fragility of Value under Imperfect Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28881)
*Winter Cross*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文建立了对齐问题的数学模型，研究了在理想化对齐训练后，代理的代理价值函数在何种条件下会在优化能力趋于无穷时导致灾难性后果（人类期望价值低于η），并主张应设计限制优化压力的AI系统（如quantilizers）而非仅依赖部署前训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统承担越来越多责任，确保其与人类价值观对齐变得至关重要。AI安全领域普遍担忧"人类价值脆弱性"——即过度优化人类价值的不完美代理函数可能导致灾难性后果。本文旨在通过形式化建模来严格刻画这种风险的条件。

Method: 本文构建了一个对齐问题的数学模型，其中代理经过理想化的对齐训练，保证其价值函数满足某种代理条件后才会优化世界。通过分析人类价值函数的性质以及多种代理条件的准确性，识别出在什么条件下一个具有η-灾难性价值函数（即保证在优化功率极限下将人类价值期望推至η以下的函数）的代理会被部署。

Result: 本文给出了在何种条件下，具有η-灾难性价值函数的代理会被部署的充要条件或充分条件，揭示了过度优化（overoptimization）的危险性。

Conclusion: 研究结果警示了过度优化的风险，并论证了应该设计限制优化压力的AI架构（如quantilizers），而非仅依赖部署前训练来保证安全性。

Abstract: As more responsibility is placed upon AI systems, it becomes increasingly important to guarantee that these systems are aligned with humanity. A common fear in AI safety is that human value is fragile -- that is, optimizing too heavily for an imperfect proxy to human values will lead to a catastrophic outcome. In this paper, we present a model of the alignment problem where an agent undergoes idealized alignment training that guarantees its value function satisfies a proxy condition before optimizing the world. Our primary results identify conditions on the human value function and the accuracy of several proxy conditions under which an agent with an $η$-catastrophic value function, one that is guaranteed to take the expectation of human value below $η$ in the limit of optimizing power, would be deployed. Our results highlight the danger of overoptimization and motivate AI designs that limit optimization pressure, such as quantilizers, rather than relying solely on pre-deployment training.

</details>


### [94] [Identifying Informative Environments for Cognition Parameter Inference via Bayesian Experimental Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28894)
*Manisha Dubey,Rimvydas Rubavicius,N. Siddharth,Subramanian Ramamoorthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将认知规划实验的设计问题形式化为贝叶斯实验设计（BED）问题，将实验环境作为设计变量，并引入摊销贝叶斯实验设计框架进行高效的后验推断和设计评估。在Mouselab-MDP过程追踪范式上的实验表明，摊销BED与精确蒙特卡洛BED在环境排名上高度一致，同时大幅降低了计算成本。研究还发现不存在对所有认知推断目标都最优的单一环境，揭示了期望信息增益、后验可恢复性和信息效率之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算认知建模旨在从可观测行为中推断潜在认知机制，贝叶斯逆规划为此提供了原则性框架，但其成功高度依赖于实验环境。现有方法通常将实验环境视为固定的，未回答"哪种认知实验对认知参数推断最具信息量"这一关键问题，因此需要一种系统化的方法来设计最优的认知规划实验环境。

Method: 1) 将认知规划实验设计形式化为贝叶斯实验设计问题，以实验环境作为设计变量；2) 建立精确蒙特卡洛BED基准；3) 引入摊销贝叶斯实验设计框架，通过训练神经网络实现高效的后验推断和设计评估；4) 在Mouselab-MDP过程追踪范式上进行实验验证。

Result: 摊销BED在环境排名上与精确蒙特卡洛BED高度一致，但计算成本大幅降低。此外，不存在对多个认知推断目标（期望信息增益、后验可恢复性、信息效率）均最优的单一实验环境，各目标之间存在权衡关系。

Conclusion: 该研究为设计信息量丰富的认知实验提供了原则性框架，支持贝叶斯参数推断，揭示了实验设计中对不同推断目标的权衡，为计算认知建模领域的实验设计提供了新的方法论工具。

Abstract: Computational cognitive modeling seeks to infer latent cognitive mechanisms underlying observed behavior. Bayesian inverse planning provides a principled framework for such inference, but its success depends critically on the experimental environment. Existing approaches typically treat environments as fixed, leaving open the question of which cognitive experiments are most informative for cognition parameter inference. We formulate the design of cognitive planning experiments as a Bayesian Experimental Design (BED) problem, treating the experimental environment as the design variable. We establish an exact Monte Carlo BED benchmark and introduce an amortized Bayesian experimental design framework for efficient posterior inference and design evaluation. Experiments on the Mouselab-MDP process-tracing paradigm show that amortized BED closely matches the environment rankings of exact Monte Carlo BED while substantially reducing computational cost. We further show that no single environment is uniformly optimal across cognitive inference objectives, revealing trade-offs between expected information gain, posterior recoverability, and information efficiency. These results provide a principled framework for designing informative cognitive experiments for Bayesian parameter inference.

</details>


### [95] [NeSyFS: A Neuro-symbolic Fast-Slow Thinking Framework for LLM Agent under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28942)
*Duo Xu,Faramarz Fekri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号快慢思维框架（NeSyFS），通过知识图谱表示信念状态，结合快速反应模块与基于扭曲序贯蒙特卡洛（TSMC）的不确定性感知慢速规划模块，统一解决LLM智能体在部分可观测环境中的信念推断、目标错位和不确定性规划问题，在ALFWorld、Webshop和ScienceWorld基准上取得显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在部分可观测环境中面临信念状态推断、任务目标错位和不确定性规划三大挑战。现有方法通常将动作直接建立在完整或摘要化的动作-观测历史上，其中冗余和无关信息会误导LLM的决策。作者受人类认知中快慢思维启发，希望以统一方式解决这些挑战。

Method: 提出NeSyFS框架，包含三个核心模块：1）用知识图谱（KG）以三元组形式表示信念状态，为每个模块提供上下文；2）快思维模块执行反应式动作；3）慢思维模块遵循扭曲序贯蒙特卡洛（TSMC）算法的高层结构进行不确定性感知规划；4）反思模块用于反思快思维动作并纠正任务目标错位，同时当反应式动作反复失败时切换到慢思维模块。

Result: 在ALFWorld、Webshop和ScienceWorld三个代表性基准上的实验表明，NeSyFS相较于先前方法具有显著优势。

Conclusion: NeSyFS通过神经符号的快慢思维协作方式，有效应对了部分可观测环境下LLM智能体的决策挑战，验证了知识图谱表示信念状态和自适应切换快慢思维的有效性。

Abstract: Recently Large Language Models (LLMs) have been increasingly deployed as autonomous agents in applications such as self-reflection, retrieval-augmented generation, and scientific discovery. In these settings, agents must act based on limited observations rather than full environmental states, leading to partial observability. This introduces several key challenges: belief state inference, task objective misalignment, and planning under uncertainty. Prior approaches typically condition actions on full or summarized action-observation histories whose redundant and irrelevant information can mislead the decision making of LLM agent. Inspired by human cognition, we propose a novel neuro-symbolic fast-slow thinking (NeSyFS) framework for LLM agent, addressing the challenges introduced by partial observability in a unified approach. We use a knowledge graph (KG) to represent the belief state, providing triplets as context for every module of NeSyFS. The fast-thinking module performs reactive action, while slow-thinking conducts a new uncertainty-aware planning by following the high-level structure of twisted sequential Monte Carlo (TSMC) algorithm. To mitigate the misalignment of task objective, a reflection module is used to reflect fast-thinking actions, and also switches to the slow-thinking module whenever reactive actions repeatedly fail. Experiments on three representative benchmarks, i.e. ALFWorld, Webshop, and ScienceWorld, demonstrate significant advantages over previous methods.

</details>


### [96] [MerchantBench: Benchmarking LLM Agents for Long-Term Coherence in E-Commerce Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28956)
*Qiming Shi,Yulong Tao,Linbo Jin,Zhaolu Kang,Yibo Dou,Jiawen Zhu,Tianjun Pan,Shaokang Fu,Chengyu Wang,Siyue Li,Yaping Cheng,Di Weng,Chengfu Huo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MerchantBench，一个基于真实电商数据的365天订单级模拟基准，用于评估大语言模型智能体在长期连贯性（Long-Term Coherence）方面的能力。实验表明最先进的LLM配置仅达到人类参与者平均最终净资产的27.3%，揭示了当前LLM在长期目标保持与延迟反馈适应方面的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体基准大多聚焦于有界任务和即时成功标准，无法评估真实部署中所需的长期连贯性——即在扩展时间范围内保持目的性行为并基于累积证据调整决策的能力。评估这种能力需要一个持久环境，其中行动约束未来选择、反馈存在异构延迟、且不一致行为产生可测量的累积效应。

Method: 作者构建了MerchantBench，一个365天的订单级模拟环境，基于98,843条真实电商产品记录，配备26个工具。该模拟涵盖产品采购、上架与定价控制、现金流管理、混合延迟反馈适应等相互依赖的决策任务。环境将即时可观察的上游供应商事件与延迟的下游订单结果耦合，要求智能体追踪单个订单生命周期并回访早期决策。研究对8个LLM在2种智能体框架下进行了48次运行评估（每次跨越365个模拟日）。

Result: 实验结果显示，即使是最先进的LLM与人类参与者之间仍存在巨大差距，最佳LLM配置仅达到人类参与者平均最终净资产的27.3%。这表明当前LLM在长期连贯性这一关键能力上显著不足。

Conclusion: MerchantBench揭示了LLM智能体在长期连贯性上的显著缺口，为评估通用智能体的持久目标保持、延迟反馈适应和累积决策能力提供了一个切实可行的基准框架。该研究强调了从有界任务评估转向长期、动态、真实世界场景评估的必要性。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly evaluated as autonomous tool users, yet most benchmarks focus on bounded tasks with immediate success criteria. Real-world deployments often require Long-Term Coherence, the capacity to preserve purposeful behavior across extended horizons while adapting decisions to accumulated evidence. Evaluating this capacity requires a persistent environment in which actions constrain future choices, feedback arrives at heterogeneous delays, and incoherent behavior produces measurable cumulative effects. Seller-side e-commerce provides a suitable setting for this evaluation through recurrent and interdependent decisions over Product Sourcing, Listing and Pricing Control, Cash-Flow Management, and Mixed-Latency Feedback Adaptation. We introduce MerchantBench, a 365-day order-level simulation grounded in 98,843 real e-commerce product records and equipped with 26 tools for agent interaction. MerchantBench couples promptly observable Upstream Supplier Events with delayed Downstream Order Outcomes, requiring agents to follow individual order lifecycles and revisit earlier decisions. We evaluate eight LLMs under two agent frameworks in 48 runs, each spanning 365 simulated days. Our results reveal a substantial gap between even the latest LLMs and human participants, with the best LLM configuration attaining only 27.3\% of the mean final net assets achieved by human participants.

</details>


### [97] [Scaling Scientific Discovery Environments for Turn-Level Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28990)
*Yucheng Xu,Keyi Zhang,Yuyang Yu,Min Zhang,Shiyuan Meng,Pei Chu,Zhongying Tu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SciDisco框架，通过构建过程可验证的科学发现环境来训练大语言模型智能体，使其在长时程科学数据分析任务中达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体在数据驱动的科学发现任务中展现出潜力，但长时程科学分析受限于缺乏对真实科学数据进行过程监督（process supervision）的训练环境，导致智能体在长期交互中难以获得中间步骤的反馈信号。

Method: SciDisco框架包含三个核心组件：1）SciThèque——将假设、数据集、隐藏证据图和验证器编译为任务环境，在交互过程中可实时检查分析进展；2）DAG-grounded轨迹合成——利用这些环境通过验证器过滤生成多轮演示；3）DiscoPO——以环境为训练信号来源，对产生可验证分析证据的动作进行回合级信用分配。

Result: SciDisco-14B在假设驱动的科学数据分析基准上达到了最先进水平（state-of-the-art）。

Conclusion: 通过过程可验证环境的构建和训练，SciDisco有效解决了科学发现智能体中缺乏过程监督的问题，验证了在真实科学数据环境中进行可验证训练信号的可行性。

Abstract: Large language model agents have shown promising capabilities in data-driven scientific discovery tasks, where an agent interacts with an execution environment and produces a statistical claim. Long-horizon scientific analysis remains constrained by the lack of process supervised environments over real-world scientific data. This paper introduces SciDisco, a scalable framework for training Scientific Discovery agents in process-verifiable environments. SciThèque compiles hypotheses, datasets, hidden evidence graphs, and verifiers into task environments where analytical progress can be checked during interaction. DAG-grounded trajectory synthesis uses these environments to construct verifier-filtered multi-turn demonstrations. DiscoPO then uses the environment as the source of training signal, assigning turn-level credit to actions that produce verifiable analytical evidence. Experiments show that SciDisco-14B reaches state-of-the-art on hypothesis-driven scientific data analysis benchmarks.

</details>


### [98] [MMShopBench: A Real-Log Benchmark for Multimodal, Multi-Turn Shopping Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29002)
*Zeying Hao,Hao Guo,Mengtao Xu,Yimin Hu,Yuheng Song,Zesheng Zhou,Jinsong Lan,Xiaoyong Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Online shoppers increasingly turn to AI shopping assistants, using images and multi-turn dialogue to express and refine product needs that are difficult to articulate in text alone. However, existing benchmarks largely rely on text-only or synthetic requests, underrepresenting complex real-world shopping requirements jointly expressed through images and language. We introduce MMShopBench, the first real-log benchmark for multimodal, multi-turn shopping agents. Built from carefully cleaned and manually annotated shopping logs, MMShopBench provides ground-truth annotations of each request's purchase intent and mandatory product requirements. Agents must infer these requirements jointly from user images and multi-turn dialogue, retrieve candidate products through image and text search, and verify that each candidate satisfies all requirements using its product images and structured attributes. We evaluate representative open-source and proprietary models using an evidence-grounded multimodal protocol and construct a companion training set for fine-tuning an open-source model. To ensure reproducible experimentation, we build an offline shopping sandbox, where fine-tuning substantially narrows the performance gap between our open-source model and leading proprietary models, demonstrating the effectiveness of our training data.

</details>


### [99] [Evidence-Grounded Constraint Checking in Construction Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29058)
*Rashid Mushkani,Hugo Berard,Shin Koseki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于证据的文档审查流水线，将提取的事实规范化、确定性执行四状态规则、保留源文本区间，并升级未解决案例。在29个建筑项目的160个参考任务中评估PDF证据分配器，发现将四图像预算从检索到的页面概览重新分配为一个概览加三个重叠图块，可将项目族标准化决策准确率提高10.6个百分点（p=0.031）；但在更广泛的测试块中该效应不持续，揭示了分辨率与广度之间的权衡而非区域聚焦证据的普遍优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业文档审查是一个约束检查问题，其决策依赖于文本、几何、页面和文档修订之间的复杂关系。现有方法在如何分配证据（如页面级概览与区域级图块）之间缺乏系统研究，需要明确不同证据分配策略对决策准确性的影响。

Method: 构建了一个证据基础的流水线，包含四个关键环节：规范化提取事实、确定性执行四状态规则、保留源文本区间、升级未解决案例。采用重复四系统测试和不相交两系统广度扩展两种实验设计，比较页面概览与区域图块两种证据分配策略。

Result: 重复测试中，重叠图块策略使项目族标准化决策准确率提升10.6个百分点（95% CI: 4.3-18.0，p=0.031）；但在更广泛的测试块中，Region-RAG准确率变化为-4.1点（95% CI: -10.2至1.9，p=0.209），表明效应不持久。同时，精确发现集恢复率低、假通过常见、重复运行一致性校准不佳。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了一个分辨率-广度权衡，而非区域聚焦证据的普遍优势。这推动了面向规则的证据路由和专家审查的需求，即在不同的审查场景中需要根据规则特性动态调整证据分配策略。

Abstract: Professional-document review is a constraint-checking problem in which decisions depend on relations among text, geometry, pages, and document revisions. We present an evidence-grounded pipeline that normalizes extracted facts, executes four-state rules deterministically, retains source spans, and escalates unresolved cases. We evaluate its PDF evidence allocator on 160 reference-based tasks from 29 construction projects using a repeated four-system test and a disjoint two-system breadth extension. In the repeated test, reallocating a four-image budget from retrieved page overviews to one overview and three overlapping tiles improves project-family standardized decision accuracy by 10.6 percentage points (95% project-cluster bootstrap CI: 4.3 to 18.0; exact p = 0.031). This effect does not persist in the broader block: Region-RAG changes accuracy by -4.1 points (95% CI: -10.2 to 1.9; exact p = 0.209), while an equal-image sensitivity favors page breadth. Exact finding-set recovery remains low, false passes remain common, and repeated-run agreement is poorly calibrated. The results identify a resolution-breadth trade-off rather than a universal advantage for region-focused evidence, motivating rule-aware evidence routing and expert review.

</details>


### [100] [On the Generalization of Steering Vectors for Chain-of-Thought Faithfulness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29062)
*Matthew Nguyen,Kyle Cox,Austin Meek,Iván Arcuschin*

Main category: cs.AI
